    // Р—Р°РјРµРЅР° СЃРµР»РµРєС‚Р° РЅР° С‚РµРєСЃС‚РѕРІРѕРµ РїРѕР»Рµ РїСЂРё РІС‹Р±РѕСЂРµ "РЎРІРѕР№ РІР°СЂРёР°РЅС‚"
    function handleFieldCustomChoice(pId, fieldName, val) {
        if (fieldName === 'product') {
            const patternSelect = document.getElementById(`pattern_${pId}`);
            if (val !== 'РЎРІРѕР№ РІР°СЂРёР°РЅС‚') {
                const patterns = PATTERNS_MAP[val] || ['Oversize', 'Boxy-fit', 'Regular'];
                let html = '<option value="">Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ РєСЂРѕР№...</option>';
                patterns.forEach(p => { html += `<option value="${p}">${p}</option>`; });
                html += '<option value="РЎРІРѕР№ РІР°СЂРёР°РЅС‚">+ РЎРІРѕР№ РІР°СЂРёР°РЅС‚...</option>';
                patternSelect.innerHTML = html;
            }
        } else if (fieldName === 'fabric') {
            const densitySelect = document.getElementById(`density_${pId}`);
            if (val !== 'РЎРІРѕР№ РІР°СЂРёР°РЅС‚') {
                const densities = DENSITIES_MAP[val] || [];
                let html = '<option value="">Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ РїР»РѕС‚РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ...</option>';
                densities.forEach(d => { html += `<option value="${d}">${d}</option>`; });
                html += '<option value="РЎРІРѕР№ РІР°СЂРёР°РЅС‚">+ РЎРІРѕР№ РІР°СЂРёР°РЅС‚...</option>';
                densitySelect.innerHTML = html;
            }
        }

        if (val === 'РЎРІРѕР№ РІР°СЂРёР°РЅС‚') {
            const selectEl = document.getElementById(`${fieldName}_${pId}`);
            const boxEl = document.getElementById(`custom_box_${fieldName}_${pId}`);
            const inputEl = document.getElementById(`custom_${fieldName}_${pId}`);

            selectEl.style.display = 'none';
            selectEl.removeAttribute('name');

            boxEl.style.display = 'flex';
            inputEl.setAttribute('name', `${fieldName}_${pId}`);
            inputEl.value = '';
            inputEl.focus();
        }
    }

    // Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‚ РѕС‚ С‚РµРєСЃС‚РѕРІРѕРіРѕ РїРѕР»СЏ РѕР±СЂР°С‚РЅРѕ Рє РІС‹РїР°РґР°СЋС‰РµРјСѓ СЃРїРёСЃРєСѓ
    function revertFieldToSelect(pId, fieldName) {
        const selectEl = document.getElementById(`${fieldName}_${pId}`);
        const boxEl = document.getElementById(`custom_box_${fieldName}_${pId}`);
        const inputEl = document.getElementById(`custom_${fieldName}_${pId}`);

        inputEl.removeAttribute('name');
        inputEl.value = '';
        boxEl.style.display = 'none';

        selectEl.setAttribute('name', `${fieldName}_${pId}`);
        selectEl.value = '';
        selectEl.style.display = 'block';
    }

    // РЈРјРЅРѕРµ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРѕРЅРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµ РјРµРЅСЋ: РѕС‚РєСЂС‹РІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ РІРІРµСЂС…, РµСЃР»Рё СЃРЅРёР·Сѓ РЅРµС‚ РјРµСЃС‚Р°
    function toggleStageMenu(e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        const btn = e.currentTarget || e.target.closest('button');
        const menu = document.getElementById('stageDropdownMenu');
        if (!menu) return;

        const isOpen = menu.classList.contains('active');
        if (isOpen) {
            menu.classList.remove('active');
            return;
        }

        const rect = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
        const spaceBelow = window.innerHeight - rect.bottom;
        const menuHeight = 280;

        if (spaceBelow < menuHeight && rect.top > menuHeight) {
            menu.style.top = 'auto';
            menu.style.bottom = '100%';
            menu.style.marginTop = '0';
            menu.style.marginBottom = '6px';
        } else {
            menu.style.top = '100%';
            menu.style.bottom = 'auto';
            menu.style.marginTop = '6px';
            menu.style.marginBottom = '0';
        }

        menu.classList.add('active');
    }

    document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
        const menu = document.getElementById('stageDropdownMenu');
        if (menu && !e.target.closest('#stageDropdownMenu') && !e.target.closest('button[onclick*="toggleStageMenu"]')) {
            menu.classList.remove('active');
        }
    });

    function addStageFromMenu(stageName) {
        document.getElementById('stageDropdownMenu').classList.remove('active');
        addStageRow(stageName);
    }

    function promptCustomStage() {
        document.getElementById('stageDropdownMenu').classList.remove('active');
        const name = prompt('Р’РІРµРґРёС‚Рµ РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ СЃРІРѕРµРіРѕ СЌС‚Р°РїР°:');
        if (name && name.trim()) {
            addStageRow(name.trim());
        }
    }

    function addStageRow(stageName) {
        const list = document.getElementById('sortable-stages');
        const li = document.createElement('li');
        li.className = 'stage-item-row';
        li.dataset.explicit = 'false';
        li.draggable = true;
        li.innerHTML = `
            <span style="cursor:grab; font-size:16px; color:#6b7280; padding-right:8px;" title="РџРµСЂРµС‚Р°С‰РёС‚Рµ РґР»СЏ РёР·РјРµРЅРµРЅРёСЏ РїРѕСЂСЏРґРєР°">:::</span>
            <input type="text" name="stage_type[]" value="${stageName}" readonly style="font-weight:600; flex:1; min-width:130px; border:none; background:transparent;">
            <select class="duration-select" onchange="calculateSchedule();" style="width:auto; padding:5px; font-size:13px;">
                <option value="0.25">0.25 РґРЅСЏ</option>
                <option value="0.5">0.5 РґРЅСЏ</option>
                <option value="0.75">0.75 РґРЅСЏ</option>
                <option value="1" selected>1 РґРµРЅСЊ</option>
                <option value="2">2 РґРЅСЏ</option>
                <option value="3">3 РґРЅСЏ</option>
                <option value="4">4 РґРЅСЏ</option>
                <option value="5">5 РґРЅРµР№</option>
                <option value="6">6 РґРЅРµР№</option>
                <option value="7">7 РґРЅРµР№</option>
                <option value="custom">Р’СЂСѓС‡РЅСѓСЋ (РґРЅРё)</option>
            </select>
            <input type="number" class="custom-duration" step="0.25" min="0.25" style="display:none; width:70px; padding:5px;" oninput="calculateSchedule();" placeholder="Р”РЅРµР№">
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline" style="padding:4px 8px; font-size:12px;" onclick="openIntervalPicker(this)">РРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р»</button>
            <span class="stage-dates-display" style="font-size:13px; font-weight:600; margin-left:10px; color:#58a6ff;"></span>
            
            <input type="hidden" name="start_date[]" class="start-date-input">
            <input type="hidden" name="start_q[]" class="start-q-input">
            <input type="hidden" name="end_date[]" class="end-date-input">
            <input type="hidden" name="end_q[]" class="end-q-input">
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-danger" style="padding:3px 7px; font-size:12px; margin-left:auto;" onclick="this.closest('li').remove(); calculateSchedule();">вњ•</button>
        `;
        list.appendChild(li);
        calculateSchedule();
        clearBatchStageError();
    }

    const sortList = document.getElementById('sortable-stages');
    let draggedStage = null;
    sortList.addEventListener('dragstart', e => {
        draggedStage = e.target.closest('li');
        if (draggedStage) draggedStage.classList.add('dragging');
    });
    sortList.addEventListener('dragend', e => {
        if (draggedStage) {
            draggedStage.classList.remove('dragging');
            draggedStage = null;
            calculateSchedule();
        }
    });
    sortList.addEventListener('dragover', e => {
        e.preventDefault();
        const after = getDragAfterElement(sortList, e.clientY);
        if (e.target.closest('li') !== draggedStage && draggedStage) {
            if (!after) sortList.appendChild(draggedStage);
            else sortList.insertBefore(draggedStage, after);
        }
    });
    function getDragAfterElement(container, y) {
        return [...container.querySelectorAll('li:not(.dragging)')].reduce((closest, child) => {
            const box = child.getBoundingClientRect(), offset = y - box.top - box.height / 2;
            return (offset < 0 && offset > closest.offset) ? { offset: offset, element: child } : closest;
        }, { offset: Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY }).element;
    }

    function dateToAbs(dateStr, q) { if(!dateStr) return 0; const p = dateStr.split('-'); return Math.floor(new Date(Date.UTC(p[0], p[1]-1, p[2])).getTime()/86400000) * 4 + parseInt(q) - 1; }
    function absToDateAndQ(absQ) { const dStr = new Date(Math.floor(absQ/4) * 86400000).toISOString().split('T')[0]; return { date: dStr, q: (absQ % 4) + 1 }; }
    function formatD(d) { if(!d) return ''; const p = d.split('-'); return `${p[2]}.${p[1]}.${p[0]}`; }

    let dbOccupiedMap = {};
    let localOccupiedMapForPicker = {};

    async function loadOccupiedSlots() {
        const type = document.getElementById('order_type').value;
        const res = await fetch(`/api/occupied?order_type=${type}`);
        const slots = await res.json();
        dbOccupiedMap = {};
        slots.forEach(s => {
            let start = dateToAbs(s.start_date, s.start_q);
            let end = dateToAbs(s.end_date, s.end_q);
            for (let q = start; q <= end; q++) dbOccupiedMap[q] = true;
        });
        if (type === 'sample') calculateSampleSchedule();
        else calculateSchedule();
    }

    // РџСѓРЅРєС‚ 5 РўР—: РђРІС‚РѕРјР°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРёР№ СЂР°СЃС‡РµС‚ С„РёРЅРёС€Р° РІ С„РѕСЂРјРµ
    function calculateSchedule() {
        let startD = document.getElementById('global-start-date').value;
        let startQ = document.getElementById('global-start-q').value;

        document.querySelectorAll('#sortable-stages li').forEach(row => {
            const sel = row.querySelector('.duration-select').value;
            row.querySelector('.custom-duration').style.display = (sel === 'custom') ? 'inline-block' : 'none';
        });

        if (!startD) return;

        let currentAbsQ = dateToAbs(startD, startQ);
        let localOccupiedMap = {...dbOccupiedMap};
        let lastEndObj = null;

        document.querySelectorAll('#sortable-stages li').forEach(row => {
            const sel = row.querySelector('.duration-select').value;
            let duration = (sel === 'custom') ? parseFloat(row.querySelector('.custom-duration').value || 0) : parseFloat(sel || 0);
            
            if (row.dataset.explicit === 'true') {
                let stA = dateToAbs(row.querySelector('.start-date-input').value, row.querySelector('.start-q-input').value);
                let enA = dateToAbs(row.querySelector('.end-date-input').value, row.querySelector('.end-q-input').value);
                for(let i=stA; i<=enA; i++) localOccupiedMap[i] = true;
                currentAbsQ = Math.max(currentAbsQ, enA + 1);
                lastEndObj = absToDateAndQ(enA);
            } else if (duration > 0) {
                let quartersNeeded = Math.round(duration * 4);
                let stageStartQ = null;
                let stageEndQ = null;
                
                while (quartersNeeded > 0) {
                    if (!localOccupiedMap[currentAbsQ]) {
                        if (stageStartQ === null) stageStartQ = currentAbsQ;
                        stageEndQ = currentAbsQ;
                        quartersNeeded--;
                        localOccupiedMap[currentAbsQ] = true;
                    }
                    currentAbsQ++;
                }
                
                let startObj = absToDateAndQ(stageStartQ);
                let endObj = absToDateAndQ(stageEndQ);
                lastEndObj = endObj;
                
                row.querySelector('.start-date-input').value = startObj.date;
                row.querySelector('.start-q-input').value = startObj.q;
                row.querySelector('.end-date-input').value = endObj.date;
                row.querySelector('.end-q-input').value = endObj.q;
                row.querySelector('.stage-dates-display').textContent = `${formatD(startObj.date)} (С‡.${startObj.q}) вЂ” ${formatD(endObj.date)} (С‡.${endObj.q})`;
            }
        });
        localOccupiedMapForPicker = localOccupiedMap;

        const finishEl = document.getElementById('order-finish-display');
        if (lastEndObj && finishEl) {
            finishEl.textContent = `вЂ” Р¤РёРЅРёС€: ${formatD(lastEndObj.date)}, ${lastEndObj.q}-СЏ С‡Р°СЃС‚СЊ РґРЅСЏ`;
            finishEl.style.display = 'inline';
        } else if (finishEl) {
            finishEl.style.display = 'none';
        }
    }

    function calculateSampleSchedule() {
        let startD = document.getElementById('global-start-date').value;
        let startQ = document.getElementById('global-start-q').value;
        let sel = document.getElementById('sample-duration').value;
        if (!startD) return;

        let duration = parseFloat(sel || 0);
        if (duration > 0) {
            let currentAbsQ = dateToAbs(startD, startQ);
            let quartersNeeded = Math.round(duration * 4);
            let stageEndQ = currentAbsQ;
            let localOccupiedMap = {...dbOccupiedMap};
            
            while (quartersNeeded > 0) {
                if (!localOccupiedMap[currentAbsQ]) {
                    stageEndQ = currentAbsQ;
                    quartersNeeded--;
                    localOccupiedMap[currentAbsQ] = true;
                }
                currentAbsQ++;
            }
            
            let endObj = absToDateAndQ(stageEndQ);
            document.getElementById('sample-end-date').value = endObj.date;
            document.getElementById('sample-end-q').value = endObj.q;
            document.getElementById('sample-dates-display').textContent = `${formatD(startD)} (С‡.${startQ}) вЂ” ${formatD(endObj.date)} (С‡.${endObj.q})`;
        }
    }

    function toggleOrderTypeUI() {
        const type = document.getElementById('order_type').value;
        if (type === 'sample') {
            document.getElementById('batch-ui').style.display = 'none';
            document.getElementById('sample-ui').style.display = 'block';
            calculateSampleSchedule();
        } else {
            document.getElementById('batch-ui').style.display = 'block';
            document.getElementById('sample-ui').style.display = 'none';
            calculateSchedule();
        }
    }

    function clearStartDateError() {
        const btn = document.getElementById('start-picker-btn');
        if (btn) btn.classList.remove('field-input-error');
        const group = document.getElementById('group-start-date');
        if (group) {
            const err = group.querySelector('.field-error-text');
            if (err) err.style.display = 'none';
        }
    }

    function clearBatchStageError() {
        const err = document.getElementById('batch-stages-error');
        if (err) err.style.display = 'none';
    }

    let pickerMode = 'start', pickerRow = null, currentMonth = new Date().getMonth(), currentYear = new Date().getFullYear(), selection = []; 
    function openStartPicker() {
        pickerMode = 'start'; selection = [];
        const val = document.getElementById('global-start-date').value;
        if (val) { currentYear = parseInt(val.split('-')[0]); currentMonth = parseInt(val.split('-')[1]) - 1; }
        else { currentMonth = new Date().getMonth(); currentYear = new Date().getFullYear(); }
        document.getElementById('pickerTitle').textContent = 'РќР°С‡Р°Р»Рѕ РїСЂРѕРёР·РІРѕРґСЃС‚РІР°'; 
        document.getElementById('calendarModal').classList.add('active'); 
        renderPicker();
    }
    function openIntervalPicker(btn) {
        pickerMode = 'interval'; pickerRow = btn.closest('li'); selection = [];
        const val = pickerRow.querySelector('.start-date-input').value;
        if (val) { currentYear = parseInt(val.split('-')[0]); currentMonth = parseInt(val.split('-')[1]) - 1; }
        else { currentMonth = new Date().getMonth(); currentYear = new Date().getFullYear(); }
        document.getElementById('pickerTitle').textContent = 'РРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р» СЌС‚Р°РїР°';
        document.getElementById('calendarModal').classList.add('active'); 
        renderPicker();
    }

    function closePicker() { document.getElementById('calendarModal').classList.remove('active'); }
    function changeMonth(delta) { currentMonth += delta; if (currentMonth < 0) { currentMonth = 11; currentYear--; } if (currentMonth > 11) { currentMonth = 0; currentYear++; } renderPicker(); }

    function renderPicker() {
        document.getElementById('pickerMonthYear').textContent = ['РЇРЅРІР°СЂСЊ','Р¤РµРІСЂР°Р»СЊ','РњР°СЂС‚','РђРїСЂРµР»СЊ','РњР°Р№','РСЋРЅСЊ','РСЋР»СЊ','РђРІРіСѓСЃС‚','РЎРµРЅС‚СЏР±СЂСЊ','РћРєС‚СЏР±СЂСЊ','РќРѕСЏР±СЂСЊ','Р”РµРєР°Р±СЂСЊ'][currentMonth] + ' ' + currentYear;
        const container = document.getElementById('pickerGrid'), firstDay = new Date(currentYear, currentMonth, 1).getDay(), daysInMonth = new Date(currentYear, currentMonth + 1, 0).getDate();
        let html = ''; for (let i = 0; i < ((firstDay === 0) ? 6 : firstDay - 1); i++) html += '<div></div>';
        
        for (let d = 1; d <= daysInMonth; d++) {
            const dateStr = `${currentYear}-${String(currentMonth+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(d).padStart(2,'0')}`;
            let qHtml = '';
            for (let q = 1; q <= 4; q++) {
                const cAbs = dateToAbs(dateStr, q);
                let isOcc = (pickerMode === 'start') ? dbOccupiedMap[cAbs] : localOccupiedMapForPicker[cAbs];
                let isSelected = (selection.length===1) ? (cAbs===dateToAbs(selection[0].date, selection[0].q)) : (selection.length===2 && cAbs>=Math.min(dateToAbs(selection[0].date, selection[0].q), dateToAbs(selection[1].date, selection[1].q)) && cAbs<=Math.max(dateToAbs(selection[0].date, selection[0].q), dateToAbs(selection[1].date, selection[1].q)));
                qHtml += `<div class="q-block ${isOcc?'occupied':''} ${isSelected?'selected':''}" onclick="onQClick('${dateStr}', ${q}, ${isOcc})"></div>`;
            }
            html += `<div class="day-cell-picker"><div style="font-size:12px; margin-bottom:2px; text-align:center;">${d}</div><div class="q-grid">${qHtml}</div></div>`;
        }
        container.innerHTML = html;
    }

    function onQClick(dateStr, q, isOcc) {
        if (isOcc) return; 
        if (pickerMode === 'start') selection = [{date: dateStr, q: q}];
        else {
            if (selection.length === 0 || selection.length === 2) selection = [{date: dateStr, q: q}];
            else {
                const s0Abs = dateToAbs(selection[0].date, selection[0].q), s1Abs = dateToAbs(dateStr, q);
                for(let a = Math.min(s0Abs, s1Abs); a <= Math.max(s0Abs, s1Abs); a++) {
                    if (localOccupiedMapForPicker[a]) return alert("РџРµСЂРµСЃРµС‡РµРЅРёРµ СЃ Р·Р°РЅСЏС‚С‹Рј РІСЂРµРјРµРЅРµРј!");
                }
                selection = (s1Abs < s0Abs) ? [{date: dateStr, q: q}, selection[0]] : [selection[0], {date: dateStr, q: q}];
            }
        }
        renderPicker();
    }

    function applyPicker() {
        if (selection.length === 0) return alert('Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ РІСЂРµРјСЏ');
        if (pickerMode === 'start') {
            document.getElementById('global-start-date').value = selection[0].date; 
            document.getElementById('global-start-q').value = selection[0].q;
            document.getElementById('order-start-display').textContent = `РЎС‚Р°СЂС‚: ${formatD(selection[0].date)}, ${selection[0].q}-СЏ С‡Р°СЃС‚СЊ РґРЅСЏ`; 
            clearStartDateError();

            if (document.getElementById('order_type').value === 'sample') calculateSampleSchedule();
            else calculateSchedule();
        } else {
            if (selection.length !== 2) selection.push(selection[0]);
            const row = pickerRow, st = selection[0], en = selection[1];
            row.dataset.explicit = 'true'; 
            row.querySelector('.duration-select').value = ''; 
            row.querySelector('.custom-duration').style.display = 'none';
            row.querySelector('.start-date-input').value = st.date; 
            row.querySelector('.start-q-input').value = st.q;
            row.querySelector('.end-date-input').value = en.date; 
            row.querySelector('.end-q-input').value = en.q;
            row.querySelector('.stage-dates-display').textContent = `${formatD(st.date)} (С‡.${st.q}) вЂ” ${formatD(en.date)} (С‡.${en.q})`; 
            calculateSchedule();
        }
        closePicker();
    }

    // РџСѓРЅРєС‚ 4 РўР—: Р’ РѕРєРЅРµ СЃРІРѕРґРєРё РІС‹РІРѕРґРёРј РЅР°РЅРµСЃРµРЅРёСЏ Рё С„РёРЅРёС€
    function openOrderSummaryModal() {
        let isValid = true;
        let firstInvalid = null;

        const nameInput = document.getElementById('order-name');
        if (!nameInput.value.trim()) {
            nameInput.classList.add('field-input-error');
            const group = document.getElementById('group-name');
            if (group) group.querySelector('.field-error-text').style.display = 'block';
            isValid = false;
            if (!firstInvalid) firstInvalid = nameInput;
        }

        const clientInput = document.getElementById('client_name');
        if (!clientInput.value.trim()) {
            clientInput.classList.add('field-input-error');
            isValid = false;
            if (!firstInvalid) firstInvalid = clientInput;
        }

        const startD = document.getElementById('global-start-date').value;
        const startQ = document.getElementById('global-start-q').value;
        if (!startD || !startQ) {
            const btn = document.getElementById('start-picker-btn');
            btn.classList.add('field-input-error');
            const group = document.getElementById('group-start-date');
            if (group) group.querySelector('.field-error-text').style.display = 'block';
            isValid = false;
            if (!firstInvalid) firstInvalid = btn;
        }

        const type = document.getElementById('order_type').value;
        if (type === 'batch') {
            const rows = document.querySelectorAll('#sortable-stages li');
            if (!rows.length) {
                const err = document.getElementById('batch-stages-error');
                if (err) err.style.display = 'block';
                isValid = false;
                if (!firstInvalid) firstInvalid = document.getElementById('sortable-stages');
            }
        }

        if (!isValid) {
            if (firstInvalid) firstInvalid.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'center' });
            return;
        }

        const num = document.getElementById('order_number').value;
        const name = nameInput.value.trim();
        const client = clientInput.value.trim();
        const dateCreated = document.getElementById('creation_date').value;
        const amount = document.getElementById('order_amount').value || 0;
        const comment = document.getElementById('order-comment').value.trim();
        const totalQty = document.getElementById('total-order-qty').textContent;

        let positionsHtml = '';
        document.querySelectorAll('.position-card').forEach((c, idx) => {
            const pId = c.dataset.pid;
            const prod = c.querySelector(`[name="product_${pId}"]`).value;
            const pat = c.querySelector(`[name="pattern_${pId}"]`).value;
            const fab = c.querySelector(`[name="fabric_${pId}"]`).value;
            const den = c.querySelector(`[name="density_${pId}"]`).value;
            const col = c.querySelector(`[name="color_${pId}"]`).value;
            const posQty = c.querySelector(`#pos-total-display-${pId}`).textContent;

            // РќР°РЅРµСЃРµРЅРёСЏ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё РґР»СЏ СЃРІРѕРґРєРё
            let embList = [];
            c.querySelectorAll('.emb-row').forEach(er => {
                const et = er.querySelector(`[name="emb_type_${pId}[]"]`).value;
                const ef = er.querySelector(`[name="emb_format_${pId}[]"]`).value;
                const eq = er.querySelector(`[name="emb_quantity_${pId}[]"]`).value;
                embList.push(`${et} (${ef}) вЂ” ${eq} С€С‚.`);
            });

            positionsHtml += `
                <div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.03); border:1px solid #30363d; border-radius:6px; padding:10px; margin-bottom:8px;">
                    <div style="font-weight:600; color:#58a6ff; margin-bottom:4px;">РџРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ #${idx+1}: ${prod || 'вЂ”'} (${posQty})</div>
                    <div style="font-size:12px; color:#8b949e;">
                        <strong>РљСЂРѕР№:</strong> ${pat || 'вЂ”'} | <strong>РџРѕР»РѕС‚РЅРѕ:</strong> ${fab || 'вЂ”'} | <strong>РџР»РѕС‚РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ:</strong> ${den || 'вЂ”'} | <strong>Р¦РІРµС‚:</strong> ${col || 'вЂ”'}
                    </div>
                    ${embList.length ? `<div style="font-size:12px; margin-top:4px; color:#f59e0b;"><strong>РќР°РЅРµСЃРµРЅРёСЏ:</strong> ${embList.join(', ')}</div>` : ''}
                </div>
            `;
        });

        let stagesHtml = '';
        let lastEndText = '';
        document.querySelectorAll('#sortable-stages li').forEach(s => {
            const sName = s.querySelector('input[name="stage_type[]"]').value;
            const sDates = s.querySelector('.stage-dates-display').textContent;
            stagesHtml += `<li><strong>${sName}:</strong> ${sDates}</li>`;
            lastEndText = s.querySelector('.end-date-input').value;
        });

        const finishDisplay = document.getElementById('order-finish-display').textContent;

        const summaryHtml = `
            <div style="margin-bottom:15px; background:rgba(37,99,235,0.06); padding:12px; border-radius:6px;">
                <div style="font-size:16px; font-weight:700; margin-bottom:6px;">Р—Р°РєР°Р· #${num} вЂ” ${name}</div>
                <div><strong>РљР»РёРµРЅС‚ / Р‘СЂРµРЅРґ:</strong> ${client}</div>
                <div><strong>Р”Р°С‚Р° РѕС„РѕСЂРјР»РµРЅРёСЏ:</strong> ${formatD(dateCreated)}</div>
                <div><strong>РЎСѓРјРјР° Р·Р°РєР°Р·Р°:</strong> ${amount} в‚Ѕ</div>
                <div><strong>РћР±С‰РёР№ С‚РёСЂР°Р¶:</strong> ${totalQty} С€С‚.</div>
                ${comment ? `<div><strong>РљРѕРјРјРµРЅС‚Р°СЂРёР№:</strong> ${comment}</div>` : ''}
            </div>

            <h4 style="margin-bottom:8px;">РЎРѕСЃС‚Р°РІ РёР·РґРµР»РёР№:</h4>
            ${positionsHtml || '<p style="color:#8b949e;">РџРѕР·РёС†РёРё РЅРµ РґРѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅС‹</p>'}

            <h4 style="margin-top:15px; margin-bottom:8px;">Р“СЂР°С„РёРє РїСЂРѕРёР·РІРѕРґСЃС‚РІР°:</h4>
            <div style="margin-bottom:6px;">
                <strong>${document.getElementById('order-start-display').textContent}</strong> 
                ${finishDisplay ? `<span style="color:#58a6ff; font-weight:600;"> ${finishDisplay}</span>` : ''}
            </div>
            <ul style="padding-left:20px; margin:0;">${stagesHtml}</ul>
        `;

        document.getElementById('summaryContent').innerHTML = summaryHtml;
        document.getElementById('summaryModal').classList.add('active');
    }

    function closeSummaryModal() {
        document.getElementById('summaryModal').classList.remove('active');
    }

    function submitOrderFormFinal() {
        isOrderSubmitted = true;
        document.getElementById('orderForm').submit();
    }

    // Р–РёРІРѕРµ Р°РІС‚РѕСЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёРµ С‡РµСЂРЅРѕРІРёРєР° Рё РІРѕСЃСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРµ
    let currentDraftId = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('draft_id') || null;
    let isOrderSubmitted = false;
    let autoSaveTimer = null;

    if (currentDraftId) {
        const inp = document.getElementById('loaded_draft_id');
        if (inp) inp.value = currentDraftId;
    }

    function hasAnyFormData() {
        const name = (document.getElementById('order-name') ? document.getElementById('order-name').value.trim() : '');
        const client = (document.getElementById('client_name') ? document.getElementById('client_name').value.trim() : '');
        const comment = (document.getElementById('order-comment') ? document.getElementById('order-comment').value.trim() : '');
        const contact = (document.getElementById('contact_input') ? document.getElementById('contact_input').value.trim() : '');
        const amount = (document.getElementById('order_amount') ? parseFloat(document.getElementById('order_amount').value) || 0 : 0);
        const stagesCount = document.querySelectorAll('#sortable-stages li').length;
        const totalQty = parseInt(document.getElementById('total-order-qty').textContent, 10) || 0;

        let hasSelectedOption = false;
        document.querySelectorAll('.position-card select').forEach(s => {
            if (s.value) hasSelectedOption = true;
        });

        return Boolean(name || client || comment || contact || amount > 0 || stagesCount > 0 || totalQty > 0 || hasSelectedOption);
    }

    function getDraftTitle() {
        const name = (document.getElementById('order-name') ? document.getElementById('order-name').value.trim() : '');
        const client = (document.getElementById('client_name') ? document.getElementById('client_name').value.trim() : '');
        const num = (document.getElementById('order_number') ? document.getElementById('order_number').value : '');
        if (name) return name;
        if (client) return 'Р—Р°РєР°Р· ' + client;
        return 'Р§РµСЂРЅРѕРІРёРє #' + (num || '1');
    }

    function serializeFormState() {
        const formEl = document.getElementById('orderForm');
        const formData = new FormData(formEl);
        const fields = {};
        for (let [k, v] of formData.entries()) {
            if (k !== 'order_files' && k !== 'loaded_draft_id' && !k.startsWith('pos_files_')) {
                if (k.endsWith('[]')) {
                    if (!fields[k]) fields[k] = [];
                    fields[k].push(v);
                } else {
                    fields[k] = v;
                }
            }
        }
        return fields;
    }

    async function saveCurrentAsDraft(isSilent = false) {
        if (isOrderSubmitted) return;
        if (!hasAnyFormData()) {
            if (!isSilent) alert('Р¤РѕСЂРјР° РїСѓСЃС‚Р°, СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏС‚СЊ РЅРµС‡РµРіРѕ.');
            return;
        }

        const draftObj = {
            draft_id: currentDraftId,
            title: getDraftTitle(),
            fields: serializeFormState()
        };

        const payload = JSON.stringify(draftObj);

        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/drafts', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: payload,
                keepalive: true
            });
            const data = await res.json();
            if (data && data.success) {
                currentDraftId = data.draft_id;
                const inp = document.getElementById('loaded_draft_id');
                if (inp) inp.value = currentDraftId;
                if (!isSilent) alert('Р§РµСЂРЅРѕРІРёРє СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅС‘РЅ!');
            }
        } catch(e) {
            if (!isSilent) alert('РћС€РёР±РєР° РїСЂРё СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅРµРЅРёРё С‡РµСЂРЅРѕРІРёРєР°');
        }
    }

    function scheduleAutoSave() {
        if (isOrderSubmitted) return;
        clearTimeout(autoSaveTimer);
        autoSaveTimer = setTimeout(() => {
            if (hasAnyFormData()) {
                saveCurrentAsDraft(true);
            }
        }, 1000);
    }

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        loadOccupiedSlots();
        addPositionCard();

        const formEl = document.getElementById('orderForm');
        if (formEl) {
            formEl.addEventListener('input', scheduleAutoSave);
            formEl.addEventListener('change', scheduleAutoSave);
        }

        document.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            const link = e.target.closest('a');
            if (link && link.href && !link.target && !link.hasAttribute('download')) {
                if (!isOrderSubmitted && hasAnyFormData()) {
                    saveCurrentAsDraft(true);
                }
            }
        });

        document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
            if (document.visibilityState === 'hidden' && !isOrderSubmitted && hasAnyFormData()) {
                saveCurrentAsDraft(true);
            }
        });
        window.addEventListener('pagehide', () => {
            if (!isOrderSubmitted && hasAnyFormData()) {
                saveCurrentAsDraft(true);
            }
        });

        setTimeout(loadDraftIfPresent, 250);
    });

    async function loadDraftIfPresent() {
        if (!currentDraftId) return;
        try {
            const res = await fetch('/api/drafts/' + currentDraftId);
            const draft = await res.json();
            if (!draft || !draft.data_json) return;
            const data = JSON.parse(draft.data_json);
            const f = data.fields || {};

            if (f.order_number) document.getElementById('order_number').value = f.order_number;
            if (f.name) document.getElementById('order-name').value = f.name;
            if (f.client_name) document.getElementById('client_name').value = f.client_name;
            if (f.creation_date) document.getElementById('creation_date').value = f.creation_date;
            if (f.amount) document.getElementById('order_amount').value = f.amount;
            if (f.order_type) {
                document.getElementById('order_type').value = f.order_type;
                toggleOrderTypeUI();
            }
            if (f.contact) document.getElementById('contact_input').value = f.contact;
            if (f.comment) document.getElementById('order-comment').value = f.comment;
            if (f.order_start_date) {
                document.getElementById('global-start-date').value = f.order_start_date;
                document.getElementById('global-start-q').value = f.order_start_q || 1;
                document.getElementById('order-start-display').textContent = `РЎС‚Р°СЂС‚: ${formatD(f.order_start_date)}, ${f.order_start_q || 1}-СЏ С‡Р°СЃС‚СЊ РґРЅСЏ`;
            }
        } catch(e) {}
    }
</script>
{% endblock %}

        function toggleDarkMode() {
            document.body.classList.toggle('dark-theme');
            localStorage.setItem('theme', document.body.classList.contains('dark-theme') ? 'dark' : 'light');
        }
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
            if (localStorage.getItem('theme') === 'dark') {
                document.body.classList.add('dark-theme');
            }
        });

        function openDevModal() { document.getElementById('devModal').classList.add('active'); }
        function closeDevModal() { document.getElementById('devModal').classList.remove('active'); }

        function openOrderModal(orderId) {
            fetch('/order/' + orderId).then(r => r.json()).then(data => {
                if (data.error) { alert(data.error); return; }
                const body = document.getElementById('orderModalBody');

                        </div>
                    `).join('');
                }

                const commentHtml = data.comment ? `
                    <div style="margin-bottom: 15px; padding: 10px 12px; background: rgba(245,158,11,0.1); border-left: 3px solid #f59e0b; border-radius: 4px; font-size: 13px;">
                        <strong>РљРѕРјРјРµРЅС‚Р°СЂРёР№:</strong> ${data.comment}

                ` : '';

                        <strong>РљРѕРЅС‚Р°РєС‚:</strong> ${data.contact || 'РќРµ СѓРєР°Р·Р°РЅ'} <br>

                    </p>
                    
                    ${commentHtml}
                    
                    <div style="background: rgba(37,99,235,0.08); padding: 12px; border-radius: 6px; margin-bottom: 15px;">
                        <p style="margin-bottom: 4px;"><strong>РЎС‚Р°СЂС‚ РїСЂРѕРёР·РІРѕРґСЃС‚РІР°:</strong> ${data.start_date} (С‡.${data.start_q})</p>

                            <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline" style="padding:4px 8px; font-size:12px;" onclick="openIntervalPicker(this)">РРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р»</button>

<script>
    async function checkOrderNumber(val) {
        const statusEl = document.getElementById('order-number-status');
        if (!val || val <= 0) { statusEl.textContent = ''; return; }
        const res = await fetch(`/api/check_order_number?number=${val}&exclude_id={{ order.id }}`);
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.taken) {
            statusEl.textContent = 'РќРѕРјРµСЂ СѓР¶Рµ Р·Р°РЅСЏС‚!';
        } else {
            statusEl.textContent = '';
        }
    }

    function toggleStageMenu(e) {
        e.stopPropagation();
        document.getElementById('stageDropdownMenu').classList.toggle('active');
    }
    document.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const menu = document.getElementById('stageDropdownMenu');
        if (menu) menu.classList.remove('active');
    });

    function addStageFromMenu(stageName) {
        document.getElementById('stageDropdownMenu').classList.remove('active');
        addStageRow(stageName);
    }
    function promptCustomStage() {
        document.getElementById('stageDropdownMenu').classList.remove('active');
        const name = prompt('Р’РІРµРґРёС‚Рµ РЅР°Р·РІР°РЅРёРµ СЌС‚Р°РїР°:');
        if (name && name.trim()) addStageRow(name.trim());
    }

    function addStageRow(stageName) {
        const list = document.getElementById('sortable-stages');
        const li = document.createElement('li');
        li.className = 'stage-item-row';
        li.dataset.explicit = 'false';
        li.draggable = true;
        li.innerHTML = `
            <span style="cursor:grab; font-size:16px; color:#6b7280; padding-right:8px;">:::</span>
            <input type="text" name="stage_type[]" value="${stageName}" readonly style="font-weight:600; flex:1; min-width:130px; border:none; background:transparent;">
            <select class="duration-select" onchange="calculateSchedule();" style="width:auto; padding:5px; font-size:13px;">
                <option value="0.25">0.25 РґРЅСЏ</option>
                <option value="0.5">0.5 РґРЅСЏ</option>
                <option value="0.75">0.75 РґРЅСЏ</option>
                <option value="1" selected>1 РґРµРЅСЊ</option>
                <option value="2">2 РґРЅСЏ</option>
                <option value="3">3 РґРЅСЏ</option>
                <option value="4">4 РґРЅСЏ</option>
                <option value="5">5 РґРЅРµР№</option>
                <option value="6">6 РґРЅРµР№</option>
                <option value="7">7 РґРЅРµР№</option>
                <option value="custom">Р’СЂСѓС‡РЅСѓСЋ (РґРЅРё)</option>
            </select>
            <input type="number" class="custom-duration" step="0.25" min="0.25" style="display:none; width:70px; padding:5px;" oninput="calculateSchedule();" placeholder="Р”РЅРµР№">
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline" style="padding:4px 8px; font-size:12px;" onclick="openIntervalPicker(this)">РРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р»</button>
            <span class="stage-dates-display" style="font-size:13px; font-weight:600; margin-left:10px; color:#58a6ff;"></span>
            
            <input type="hidden" name="start_date[]" class="start-date-input">
            <input type="hidden" name="start_q[]" class="start-q-input">
            <input type="hidden" name="end_date[]" class="end-date-input">
            <input type="hidden" name="end_q[]" class="end-q-input">
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-danger" style="padding:3px 7px; font-size:12px; margin-left:auto;" onclick="this.closest('li').remove(); calculateSchedule();">вњ•</button>
        `;
        list.appendChild(li);
        calculateSchedule();
    }

    function dateToAbs(dateStr, q) { if(!dateStr) return 0; const p = dateStr.split('-'); return Math.floor(new Date(Date.UTC(p[0], p[1]-1, p[2])).getTime()/86400000) * 4 + parseInt(q) - 1; }
    function absToDateAndQ(absQ) { const dStr = new Date(Math.floor(absQ/4) * 86400000).toISOString().split('T')[0]; return { date: dStr, q: (absQ % 4) + 1 }; }
    function formatD(d) { if(!d) return ''; const p = d.split('-'); return `${p[2]}.${p[1]}.${p[0]}`; }

    let dbOccupiedMap = {};
    let localOccupiedMapForPicker = {};

    async function loadOccupiedSlots() {
        const type = document.getElementById('order_type').value;
        const res = await fetch(`/api/occupied?order_type=${type}&exclude_id={{ order.id }}`);
        const slots = await res.json();
        dbOccupiedMap = {};
        slots.forEach(s => {
            let start = dateToAbs(s.start_date, s.start_q);
            let end = dateToAbs(s.end_date, s.end_q);
            for (let q = start; q <= end; q++) dbOccupiedMap[q] = true;
        });
        calculateSchedule();
    }

    function calculateSchedule() {
        let startD = document.getElementById('global-start-date').value;
        let startQ = document.getElementById('global-start-q').value;

        document.querySelectorAll('#sortable-stages li').forEach(row => {
            const sel = row.querySelector('.duration-select').value;
            row.querySelector('.custom-duration').style.display = (sel === 'custom') ? 'inline-block' : 'none';
        });

        if (!startD) return;

        let currentAbsQ = dateToAbs(startD, startQ);
        let localOccupiedMap = {...dbOccupiedMap};
        let lastEndObj = null;

        document.querySelectorAll('#sortable-stages li').forEach(row => {
            const sel = row.querySelector('.duration-select').value;
            let duration = (sel === 'custom') ? parseFloat(row.querySelector('.custom-duration').value || 0) : parseFloat(sel || 0);
            
            if (row.dataset.explicit === 'true' && row.querySelector('.start-date-input').value) {
                let stA = dateToAbs(row.querySelector('.start-date-input').value, row.querySelector('.start-q-input').value);
                let enA = dateToAbs(row.querySelector('.end-date-input').value, row.querySelector('.end-q-input').value);
                for(let i=stA; i<=enA; i++) localOccupiedMap[i] = true;
                currentAbsQ = Math.max(currentAbsQ, enA + 1);
                lastEndObj = absToDateAndQ(enA);
            } else if (duration > 0) {
                let quartersNeeded = Math.round(duration * 4);
                let stageStartQ = null;
                let stageEndQ = null;
                
                while (quartersNeeded > 0) {
                    if (!localOccupiedMap[currentAbsQ]) {
                        if (stageStartQ === null) stageStartQ = currentAbsQ;
                        stageEndQ = currentAbsQ;
                        quartersNeeded--;
                        localOccupiedMap[currentAbsQ] = true;
                    }
                    currentAbsQ++;
                }
                
                let startObj = absToDateAndQ(stageStartQ);
                let endObj = absToDateAndQ(stageEndQ);
                lastEndObj = endObj;
                
                row.querySelector('.start-date-input').value = startObj.date;
                row.querySelector('.start-q-input').value = startObj.q;
                row.querySelector('.end-date-input').value = endObj.date;
                row.querySelector('.end-q-input').value = endObj.q;
                row.querySelector('.stage-dates-display').textContent = `${formatD(startObj.date)} (С‡.${startObj.q}) вЂ” ${formatD(endObj.date)} (С‡.${endObj.q})`;
            }
        });
        localOccupiedMapForPicker = localOccupiedMap;

        const finishEl = document.getElementById('order-finish-display');
        if (lastEndObj && finishEl) {
            finishEl.textContent = `вЂ” Р¤РёРЅРёС€: ${formatD(lastEndObj.date)}, ${lastEndObj.q}-СЏ С‡Р°СЃС‚СЊ РґРЅСЏ`;
            finishEl.style.display = 'inline';
        }
    }

    let pickerMode = 'start', pickerRow = null, currentMonth = new Date().getMonth(), currentYear = new Date().getFullYear(), selection = []; 
    function openStartPicker() {
        pickerMode = 'start'; selection = [];
        const val = document.getElementById('global-start-date').value;
        if (val) { currentYear = parseInt(val.split('-')[0]); currentMonth = parseInt(val.split('-')[1]) - 1; }
        document.getElementById('pickerTitle').textContent = 'РќР°С‡Р°Р»Рѕ РїСЂРѕРёР·РІРѕРґСЃС‚РІР°'; 
        document.getElementById('calendarModal').classList.add('active'); 
        renderPicker();
    }
    function openIntervalPicker(btn) {
        pickerMode = 'interval'; pickerRow = btn.closest('li'); selection = [];
        const val = pickerRow.querySelector('.start-date-input').value;
        if (val) { currentYear = parseInt(val.split('-')[0]); currentMonth = parseInt(val.split('-')[1]) - 1; }
        document.getElementById('pickerTitle').textContent = 'РРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р» СЌС‚Р°РїР°';
        document.getElementById('calendarModal').classList.add('active'); 
        renderPicker();
    }
    function closePicker() { document.getElementById('calendarModal').classList.remove('active'); }
    function changeMonth(delta) { currentMonth += delta; if (currentMonth < 0) { currentMonth = 11; currentYear--; } if (currentMonth > 11) { currentMonth = 0; currentYear++; } renderPicker(); }

    function renderPicker() {
        document.getElementById('pickerMonthYear').textContent = ['РЇРЅРІР°СЂСЊ','Р¤РµРІСЂР°Р»СЊ','РњР°СЂС‚','РђРїСЂРµР»СЊ','РњР°Р№','РСЋРЅСЊ','РСЋР»СЊ','РђРІРіСѓСЃС‚','РЎРµРЅС‚СЏР±СЂСЊ','РћРєС‚СЏР±СЂСЊ','РќРѕСЏР±СЂСЊ','Р”РµРєР°Р±СЂСЊ'][currentMonth] + ' ' + currentYear;
        const container = document.getElementById('pickerGrid'), firstDay = new Date(currentYear, currentMonth, 1).getDay(), daysInMonth = new Date(currentYear, currentMonth + 1, 0).getDate();
        let html = ''; for (let i = 0; i < ((firstDay === 0) ? 6 : firstDay - 1); i++) html += '<div></div>';
        
        for (let d = 1; d <= daysInMonth; d++) {
            const dateStr = `${currentYear}-${String(currentMonth+1).padStart(2,'0')}-${String(d).padStart(2,'0')}`;
            let qHtml = '';
            for (let q = 1; q <= 4; q++) {
                const cAbs = dateToAbs(dateStr, q);
                let isOcc = (pickerMode === 'start') ? dbOccupiedMap[cAbs] : localOccupiedMapForPicker[cAbs];
                let isSelected = (selection.length===1) ? (cAbs===dateToAbs(selection[0].date, selection[0].q)) : (selection.length===2 && cAbs>=Math.min(dateToAbs(selection[0].date, selection[0].q), dateToAbs(selection[1].date, selection[1].q)) && cAbs<=Math.max(dateToAbs(selection[0].date, selection[0].q), dateToAbs(selection[1].date, selection[1].q)));
                qHtml += `<div class="q-block ${isOcc?'occupied':''} ${isSelected?'selected':''}" onclick="onQClick('${dateStr}', ${q}, ${isOcc})"></div>`;
            }
            html += `<div class="day-cell-picker"><div style="font-size:12px; margin-bottom:2px; text-align:center;">${d}</div><div class="q-grid">${qHtml}</div></div>`;
        }
        container.innerHTML = html;
    }

    function onQClick(dateStr, q, isOcc) {
        if (isOcc) return; 
        if (pickerMode === 'start') selection = [{date: dateStr, q: q}];
        else {
            if (selection.length === 0 || selection.length === 2) selection = [{date: dateStr, q: q}];
            else {
                const s0Abs = dateToAbs(selection[0].date, selection[0].q), s1Abs = dateToAbs(dateStr, q);
                for(let a = Math.min(s0Abs, s1Abs); a <= Math.max(s0Abs, s1Abs); a++) {
                    if (localOccupiedMapForPicker[a]) return alert("РџРµСЂРµСЃРµС‡РµРЅРёРµ СЃ Р·Р°РЅСЏС‚С‹Рј РІСЂРµРјРµРЅРµРј!");
                }
                selection = (s1Abs < s0Abs) ? [{date: dateStr, q: q}, selection[0]] : [selection[0], {date: dateStr, q: q}];
            }
        }
        renderPicker();
    }

    function applyPicker() {
        if (selection.length === 0) return alert('Р’С‹Р±РµСЂРёС‚Рµ РІСЂРµРјСЏ');
        if (pickerMode === 'start') {
            let oldStartD = document.getElementById('global-start-date').value;
            let oldStartQ = document.getElementById('global-start-q').value;
            let oldStartAbs = (oldStartD && oldStartQ) ? dateToAbs(oldStartD, oldStartQ) : null;
            let newStartAbs = dateToAbs(selection[0].date, selection[0].q);

            if (oldStartAbs !== null) {
                let delta = newStartAbs - oldStartAbs;
                document.querySelectorAll('#sortable-stages li').forEach(row => {
                    let stD = row.querySelector('.start-date-input').value;
                    let stQ = row.querySelector('.start-q-input').value;
                    let enD = row.querySelector('.end-date-input').value;
                    let enQ = row.querySelector('.end-q-input').value;
                    if (stD && enD) {
                        let nSt = absToDateAndQ(dateToAbs(stD, stQ) + delta);
                        let nEn = absToDateAndQ(dateToAbs(enD, enQ) + delta);
                        row.querySelector('.start-date-input').value = nSt.date;
                        row.querySelector('.start-q-input').value = nSt.q;
                        row.querySelector('.end-date-input').value = nEn.date;
                        row.querySelector('.end-q-input').value = nEn.q;
                        row.querySelector('.stage-dates-display').textContent = `${formatD(nSt.date)} (С‡.${nSt.q}) вЂ” ${formatD(nEn.date)} (С‡.${nEn.q})`;
                    }
                });
            }

            document.getElementById('global-start-date').value = selection[0].date; 
            document.getElementById('global-start-q').value = selection[0].q;
            document.getElementById('order-start-display').textContent = `РЎС‚Р°СЂС‚: ${formatD(selection[0].date)} (С‡.${selection[0].q})`; 
            calculateSchedule();
        } else {
            if (selection.length !== 2) selection.push(selection[0]);
            const row = pickerRow, st = selection[0], en = selection[1];
            row.dataset.explicit = 'true'; 
            row.querySelector('.duration-select').value = ''; 
            row.querySelector('.custom-duration').style.display = 'none';
            row.querySelector('.start-date-input').value = st.date; 
            row.querySelector('.start-q-input').value = st.q;
            row.querySelector('.end-date-input').value = en.date; 
            row.querySelector('.end-q-input').value = en.q;
            row.querySelector('.stage-dates-display').textContent = `${formatD(st.date)} (С‡.${st.q}) вЂ” ${formatD(en.date)} (С‡.${en.q})`; 
            calculateSchedule();
        }
        closePicker();
    }

    function deleteExistingFile(fileId) {
        if (!confirm('РЈРґР°Р»РёС‚СЊ СЌС‚РѕС‚ С„Р°Р№Р» РёР· Р·Р°РєР°Р·Р°?')) return;
        const form = document.createElement('form');
        form.method = 'POST';
        form.action = `/delete_file/${fileId}`;
        const input = document.createElement('input');
        input.type = 'hidden';
        input.name = 'order_id';
        input.value = '{{ order.id }}';
        form.appendChild(input);
        document.body.appendChild(form);
        form.submit();
    }

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        loadOccupiedSlots();
    });

    function stepSizeInput(btn, step) {
        const box = btn.closest('.size-cell-box');
        const inp = box.querySelector('.size-input');
        let val = parseInt(inp.value || 0, 10) || 0;
        val = Math.max(0, val + step);
        inp.value = val;
        inp.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
    }

</script>

<script>
    function handleOrderClick(orderId, event) {
        if (event) event.stopPropagation();
        openOrderModal(orderId);
    }

    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
        // Р‘РµСЃС€РѕРІРЅР°СЏ РїР»Р°РІРЅР°СЏ РїРѕРґСЃРІРµС‚РєР° РІСЃРµРіРѕ Р·Р°РєР°Р·Р° РїСЂРё РЅР°РІРµРґРµРЅРёРё РЅР° Р»СЋР±РѕРµ РїРѕР»Рµ РґРЅСЏ
        let activeHoverOrderId = null;
        const daysGrid = document.querySelector('.days-grid');

        if (daysGrid) {
            daysGrid.addEventListener('mouseover', (e) => {
                const target = e.target;
                const cell = target.closest('.q-cell[data-order-id]');
                const dayCell = target.closest('.day-cell');

                let orderId = null;
                if (cell) {
                    orderId = cell.dataset.orderId;
                } else if (dayCell) {
                    const firstOcc = dayCell.querySelector('.q-cell[data-order-id]');
                    if (firstOcc) orderId = firstOcc.dataset.orderId;
                }

                if (orderId && orderId !== activeHoverOrderId) {
                    if (activeHoverOrderId) {
                        document.querySelectorAll(`.q-cell[data-order-id="${activeHoverOrderId}"]`).forEach(c => c.classList.remove('hovered'));
                    }
                    activeHoverOrderId = orderId;
                    document.querySelectorAll(`.q-cell[data-order-id="${orderId}"]`).forEach(c => c.classList.add('hovered'));
                } else if (!orderId && activeHoverOrderId) {
                    document.querySelectorAll(`.q-cell[data-order-id="${activeHoverOrderId}"]`).forEach(c => c.classList.remove('hovered'));
                    activeHoverOrderId = null;
                }
            });

            daysGrid.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => {
                if (activeHoverOrderId) {
                    document.querySelectorAll(`.q-cell[data-order-id="${activeHoverOrderId}"]`).forEach(c => c.classList.remove('hovered'));
                    activeHoverOrderId = null;
                }
            });
        }

        document.querySelectorAll('.day-cell').forEach(dayEl => {
            dayEl.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                if (e.target.closest('.q-cell') || e.target.closest('.order-start-badge') || e.target.closest('.cross-indicators')) return;
                const occupiedCells = dayEl.querySelectorAll('.q-cell:not(.empty)');
                if (occupiedCells.length > 0) {
                    const firstOrderId = occupiedCells[0].dataset.orderId;
                    if (firstOrderId) handleOrderClick(firstOrderId, e);
                }
            });
        });
    });
</script>

from flask import Flask
import os
from app.models import database

def create_app():
    app = Flask(__name__)
    app.secret_key = 'production-calendar-secret-2024'
    
    # РџР°РїРєР° РґР»СЏ Р·Р°РіСЂСѓР·РѕРє (РјР°РєРµС‚С‹)
    app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'] = os.path.join(app.root_path, 'static', 'uploads')
    os.makedirs(app.config['UPLOAD_FOLDER'], exist_ok=True)
    
    # РРЅРёС†РёР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёСЏ Р‘Р”
    if not os.path.exists(database.DB_PATH):
        database.init_db()
    else:
        database.cleanup_db()
        database.upgrade_db()

    # Р РµРіРёСЃС‚СЂР°С†РёСЏ СЃС‚Р°СЂРѕРіРѕ С„СѓРЅРєС†РёРѕРЅР°Р»Р° (РєР°Р»РµРЅРґР°СЂСЊ)
    from app.blueprints.legacy import legacy_bp
    app.register_blueprint(legacy_bp)
    
    from app.blueprints.orders import orders_bp
    app.register_blueprint(orders_bp)
    
    return app

# РўРѕС‡РєР° РІС…РѕРґР° РґР»СЏ Gunicorn С…РѕСЃС‚РёРЅРіР°
app = create_app()

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import os
import re

add_path = 'app/templates/add_order.html'
with open(add_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    html = f.read()

# 1. РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј СЂР°Р·РјРµС‚РєСѓ РєРЅРѕРїРєРё СѓРґР°Р»РµРЅРёСЏ РІ addPositionCard РЅР° РїСЂСЏРјРѕРµ СѓРґР°Р»РµРЅРёРµ
html = html.replace(
    'onclick="removePositionCard(${pId})"',
    'onclick="removePositionCard(this)"'
)

# 2. Р’РЅРµРґСЂСЏРµРј РЅР°РґРµР¶РЅС‹Рµ С„СѓРЅРєС†РёРё СѓРґР°Р»РµРЅРёСЏ, РЅР°РЅРµСЃРµРЅРёСЏ Рё РїРµСЂРµСЃС‡РµС‚Р°
bulletproof_functions = '''
    // 100% РЅР°РґРµР¶РЅРѕРµ СѓРґР°Р»РµРЅРёРµ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё
    function removePositionCard(btn) {
        const card = btn.closest('.position-card');
        if (card) {
            card.remove();
            updateDeleteButtonsVisibility();
            recalcGlobalTotal();
        }
    }

    function updateDeleteButtonsVisibility() {
        const container = document.getElementById('positions-container');
        if (!container) return;
        const cards = container.querySelectorAll('.position-card');
        cards.forEach(c => {
            const btn = c.querySelector('.pos-delete-btn');
            if (btn) {
                btn.style.setProperty('display', cards.length > 1 ? 'inline-block' : 'none', 'important');
            }
        });
        if (cards.length > 1) {
            container.classList.add('show-delete-btn');
        } else {
            container.classList.remove('show-delete-btn');
        }
    }

    // 100% РЅР°РґРµР¶РЅРѕРµ РґРѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ РЅР°РЅРµСЃРµРЅРёСЏ
    function addEmbellishmentRow(pId) {
        const container = document.getElementById('embellishments-container-' + pId);
        if (!container) return;
        const totalEl = document.getElementById('pos-total-display-' + pId);
        const curPosTotal = parseInt(totalEl ? totalEl.textContent : 0, 10) || 0;

        const row = document.createElement('div');
        row.className = 'emb-row';
        row.innerHTML = `
            <select name="emb_type_${pId}[]" style="padding:4px; font-size:12px; flex:2;">
                <option value="DTF-РїРµС‡Р°С‚СЊ">DTF-РїРµС‡Р°С‚СЊ</option>
                <option value="РЁРµР»РєРѕРіСЂР°С„РёСЏ">РЁРµР»РєРѕРіСЂР°С„РёСЏ</option>
                <option value="РњР°С€РёРЅРЅР°СЏ РІС‹С€РёРІРєР°">РњР°С€РёРЅРЅР°СЏ РІС‹С€РёРІРєР°</option>
                <option value="DTF">DTF</option>
            </select>
            <select name="emb_format_${pId}[]" style="padding:4px; font-size:12px; flex:1;">
                <option value="A4">A4</option>
                <option value="A7">A7</option>
                <option value="A6">A6</option>
                <option value="A5">A5</option>
                <option value="A3">A3</option>
                <option value="A2">A2</option>
            </select>
            <input type="number" name="emb_quantity_${pId}[]" class="emb-qty-input" value="${curPosTotal}" min="1" placeholder="РљРѕР»-РІРѕ" style="padding:4px; font-size:12px; width:80px;" oninput="this.dataset.manualEdited='true'">
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-danger" style="padding:2px 7px; font-size:12px;" onclick="this.closest('.emb-row').remove()">вњ•</button>
        `;
        container.appendChild(row);
    }

    function recalcPositionTotal(pId) {
        const card = document.getElementById('pos-card-' + pId);
        if (!card) return 0;
        let sum = 0;
        card.querySelectorAll('.size-input').forEach(inp => {
            sum += parseInt(inp.value || 0, 10) || 0;
        });
        const posDisplay = document.getElementById('pos-total-display-' + pId);
        if (posDisplay) posDisplay.textContent = `${sum} С€С‚.`;
        
        card.querySelectorAll('.emb-qty-input').forEach(eqInp => {
            if (!eqInp.dataset.manualEdited) {
                eqInp.value = sum;
            }
        });

        recalcGlobalTotal();
        return sum;
    }

    function recalcGlobalTotal() {
        let globalSum = 0;
        document.querySelectorAll('.position-card').forEach(card => {
            card.querySelectorAll('.size-input').forEach(inp => {
                globalSum += parseInt(inp.value || 0, 10) || 0;
            });
        });
        const totalEl = document.getElementById('total-order-qty');
        if (totalEl) totalEl.textContent = globalSum;
        const hiddenTotal = document.getElementById('hidden_total_quantity');
        if (hiddenTotal) hiddenTotal.value = globalSum;
    }
'''

# РћС‡РёС‰Р°РµРј СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹Рµ РґСѓР±Р»Рё С„СѓРЅРєС†РёР№ РµСЃР»Рё Р±С‹Р»Рё
html = re.sub(r'function removePositionCard[\s\S]*?function recalcGlobalTotal\(\)\s*\{[\s\S]*?\}', '', html)

# Р’СЃС‚Р°РІР»СЏРµРј РіР°СЂР°РЅС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅРѕ РїРµСЂРµРґ РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєРѕР№ РЅРѕРјРµСЂР° Р·Р°РєР°Р·Р°
html = html.replace('async function checkOrderNumber(val) {', bulletproof_functions + '\n    async function checkOrderNumber(val) {')

with open(add_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    f.write(html)

            preferences TEXT
        )ѓ†tablestagesstagesCREATE TABLE stages (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
            order_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
            stage_type TEXT NOT NULL,
            start_date TEXT NOT NULL,
            start_q INTEGER NOT NULL,
            end_date TEXT NOT NULL,
            end_q INTEGER NOT NULL,
            FOREIGN KEY (order_id) REFERENCES orders(id) ON DELETE CASCADE
        )P++Ytablesqlite_sequencesqlite_sequenceCREATE TABLE sqlite_sequence(name,seq)Ѓkѓ1tabledraftsdraftsCREATE TABLE drafts (
            id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,

from app import create_app

app = create_app()

    port = int(os.environ.get('PORT', 5000))
    app.run(host='0.0.0.0', port=port)
